\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The compute substep: one \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-step from an
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimCompute} stage whose \isa{buf} field matches an
  \isa{M}-\isa{{\isasymdelta}}-tuple's read vector fires that tuple,
  landing at the \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimWrite} stage with \isa{M}'s
  post-step state \isa{q{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}, write vector \isa{m{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}a{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}, and
  direction vector \isa{m{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}d} threaded into the stage.  No tape
  cell changes and no head moves (the substrate write is the read
  symbol back, direction \isa{N}); the per-tape \isa{posk}
  carries through.  The two global \isa{{\isasymdelta}LE} filters are
  discharged reflexively (write equals read, move \isa{N}); the
  target-stage validity rests on \isa{valid{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}mttm}'s
  \isa{{\isasymdelta}}-range typing (\isa{m{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}a{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}\ k\ {\isasymin}\ {\isasymGamma}{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tm\ M}).
  Throughout, \isa{b} abbreviates \isa{block{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}width\ {\isasymGamma}} (the per-symbol cell width).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Displacement bound: an \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimAdvance} walk on one tape
  is at most \isa{{\isadigit{2}}b} cells (the \isa{L}-from-\isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}AtFurtherProper}
  worst case).  The \isa{{\isadigit{0}}\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ b} hypothesis is needed: the
  \isa{N}-from-\isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}AtLE} displacement is the constant \isa{{\isadigit{1}}},
  which exceeds \isa{{\isadigit{2}}b\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{0}}}.  Used to discharge target-stage validity
  (\isa{Suc\ i\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}\ {\isacharasterisk}{\kern0pt}\ b}) for the walk and boundary arms, whose
  reached bit-counter is bounded by the displacement.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
\isakeywordONE{lemma}\isamarkupfalse%
\ ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}disp{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}le{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{2}}k{\isacharcolon}{\kern0pt}\isanewline
\ \ \isakeywordTWO{assumes}\ {\isachardoublequoteopen}{\isadigit{0}}\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ k{\isachardoublequoteclose}\isanewline
\ \ \isakeywordTWO{shows}\ {\isachardoublequoteopen}ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}disp\ k\ d\ pk\ {\isasymle}\ {\isadigit{2}}\ {\isacharasterisk}{\kern0pt}\ k{\isachardoublequoteclose}\isanewline

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The advance walk substep: from an \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimAdvance} stage with
  the bit-counter strictly below the per-tape displacement, one
  \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-step moves the active tape \isa{tk}'s head one cell
  \isa{L} (\isa{N} elsewhere) and stays in \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimAdvance} at
  \isa{Suc\ i}; the tape contents and all other heads are unchanged.
  The \isa{a\ tk\ {\isasymnoteq}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}} hypothesis is load-bearing: it both selects
  this arm (\isa{{\isasymdelta}}'s stepping arm forbids an \isa{L}-walk off
  \isa{LE{\isadigit{4}}}) and discharges the backward-\isa{{\isasymdelta}LE} filter, which
  would otherwise reject the \isa{L}-move on a tape reading \isa{LE{\isadigit{4}}}.
  Target-stage validity rests on the displacement bound
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}disp{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}le{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}{\isadigit{2}}k}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The advance boundary substep, non-last tape: the final
  \isa{M{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}}-step of tape \isa{tk}'s walk, handing off to the next
  tape \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}succ\ tk} (bit-counter reset to \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}, position-kind
  updated by \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}newpos}, other tapes' \isa{posk} preserved).
  A single hypothesis covers both firing sub-cases via the arm's own
  disjunction: the \isa{R}-sub-case (\isa{dvec\ tk\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ R}, zero
  displacement, no head move) and the non-\isa{R} sub-case
  (\isa{dvec\ tk\ {\isasymnoteq}\ R}, the read not \isa{LE{\isadigit{4}}}, counter at
  \isa{Suc\ i\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ displacement}, one last \isa{L}-move).  The head
  conclusion is therefore conditional on \isa{dvec\ tk}.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The write LE-skip substep, non-last tape: when \isa{buf\ tk}
  is the left-end marker, the cell at \isa{sim{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ {\isadigit{0}}\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{0}}} is already
  \isa{LE{\isadigit{4}}} and need not be rewritten, so the per-tape write phase is
  skipped — a single all-\isa{N} substep handing off to the next tape
  \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}succ\ tk} with the bit-counter still \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}.  Tape and
  heads unchanged (the compute-step shape).%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The write back-walk substep (proper region,
  \isa{Suc\ i\ {\isasymle}\ b}): with \isa{buf\ tk} a proper symbol, the head
  first walks \isa{L} back across the \isa{b}-cell block before the
  forward-write phase; one \isa{L}-move on \isa{tk} (\isa{N}
  elsewhere), no writes, staying in \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimWrite} at \isa{Suc\ i}.
  Structurally the \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}advance{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}walk{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} shape; arm 3 of the
  union.  As there, \isa{a\ tk\ {\isasymnoteq}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}} selects the arm and discharges
  the backward-\isa{{\isasymdelta}LE} filter.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The forward-write image is never the left-end marker: it is
  \isa{BLANK{\isadigit{4}}} (blank symbol) or a bit cell of \isa{encode{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}symbol}
  (\isa{BIT{\isadigit{0}}}/\isa{BIT{\isadigit{1}}} only, by \isa{encode{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}symbol{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}cell{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}domain}),
  in range \isa{j\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ b}.  This is what makes the forward-write arms
  legal under both \isa{{\isasymdelta}LE} filters.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The \isa{j}-th cell of a block's \isa{cell{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}repr} is the
  \isa{j}-th \isa{write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit} image (\isa{j\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ b}), uniformly
  across the blank and proper branches.  This is the bridge from the
  tape-correspondence cell value (\isa{cell{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}repr\ {\isasymdots}\ {\isacharbang}{\kern0pt}\ j}) to the
  \isa{write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit} image that the write phase produces, and to the
  \isa{{\isasymnoteq}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}} fact the back-walk / bit-write guards need.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
Under the encoding, the only \isa{LE{\isadigit{4}}} cell of the simulated
  tape is at position \isa{{\isadigit{0}}}: every position \isa{{\isasymge}\ {\isadigit{1}}} lies in
  some proper block \isa{{\isacharbrackleft}{\kern0pt}sim{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ b\ p{\isacharcomma}{\kern0pt}\ sim{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}pos\ b\ p\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ b{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} (the
  \isa{div}/\isa{mod} decomposition of \isa{pos\ {\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{1}}}) and so
  carries a \isa{cell{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}repr} cell, which is never \isa{LE{\isadigit{4}}}.  The
  fact the advance back-walk's \isa{notLE} guard rests on: a head
  walking through proper cells never reads the left-end marker.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The forward-write stepping substep (proper region,
  \isa{b\ {\isasymle}\ i}, \isa{Suc\ i\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}b}): the \isa{{\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}i{\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}b{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}}-th cell of
  \isa{cell{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}repr\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}buf\ tk{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} is written at \isa{tk}, the head moves
  \isa{R} on \isa{tk} (\isa{N} elsewhere), staying in
  \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimWrite} at \isa{Suc\ i}.  First per-substep lemma that
  mutates the tape: the conclusion's \isa{mt{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}tape} is a nested
  \isa{fun{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}upd} writing \isa{write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit\ {\isasymGamma}\ bl\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}buf\ tk{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}i{\isacharminus}{\kern0pt}b{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}} at
  \isa{tk}'s head cell.  The read precondition \isa{a\ tk\ {\isasymnoteq}\ LE{\isadigit{4}}}
  (proper region is past \isa{LE{\isadigit{4}}}) discharges the backward-\isa{{\isasymdelta}LE}
  filter; \isa{write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}not{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}LE{\isadigit{4}}} discharges the forward filter.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The forward-write boundary substep, last tape: as
  \isa{ar{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}write{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}boundary{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}step} but \isa{tk} is the last tape, so

  (current-tape field reset to \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}}).  Arm 6 (rightmost) of
  the union.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The decoder image always lies in \isa{{\isasymGamma}\ {\isasymunion}\ {\isacharbraceleft}{\kern0pt}bl{\isacharbraceright}{\kern0pt}}: for
  \isa{n\ {\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ card\ {\isasymGamma}} it is \isa{inv{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}into\ {\isasymGamma}\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}gamma{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}enum\ {\isasymGamma}\ bl{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}\ n},
  which is in \isa{{\isasymGamma}} since \isa{gamma{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}enum} is onto
  \isa{{\isacharbraceleft}{\kern0pt}{\isachardot}{\kern0pt}{\isachardot}{\kern0pt}{\isacharless}{\kern0pt}\ card\ {\isasymGamma}{\isacharbraceright}{\kern0pt}} (bijection); the out-of-range fallback is
  \isa{bl}.  Totality here means the read arms' \isa{buf}-update
  validity holds for any accumulator value without per-arm range
  reasoning.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%

  into the partial decode via the \isa{gamma{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}enum}/\isa{gamma{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unenum}
  roundtrip \isa{partial{\isacharprime}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharequal}{\kern0pt}\ {\isadigit{2}}\ {\isasymcdot}\ gamma{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}enum\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}buf\ tk{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}\ {\isacharplus}{\kern0pt}\ bit{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}value\ {\isacharparenleft}{\kern0pt}a\ tk{\isacharparenright}{\kern0pt}},

\begin{isamarkuptext}%
The read proper-arm per-bit boundary substep, last tape: same
  last-bit accumulator step, transitioning to \isa{AR{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}SimCompute} with
  the current-tape field reset to \isa{k{\isacharunderscore}{\kern0pt}unidx\ {\isadigit{0}}} (all tapes decoded,
  begin the compute substep).  Arm 7 (rightmost) of the union.%
\end{isamarkuptext}\isamarkuptrue%
